## L12-1 — Account main merge and preserve its conflict resolutions

- **Date/status:** 2026-10-19; Lead direction supplied in the assignment, recorded by the Doer, not an author-selected review verdict.
- **What is happening:** merge `49ad04648447439b5c243af6ddd0b604acb5b3e5` imports main `708ea660894eb88aa4575ec07bf13d916d02c14c` into the plan branch. The Lead resolved seven conflicts. Six legacy exception digests now mismatch.
- **Cause:** upstream platform and legacy documentation changed after common base `eab4d617b06cf5e05d8602f35987a9798e67005c`; preservation requires explicit accounting, not another transformation.
- **Options/cost:** leave digests stale (ratchet blocks); re-account imported digests/main commits using `1c626c8aa`, `37f6f7499`, `c3f22d6a6` and L11-21 (six data rows, no source edit); carry/rebind now (violates A47 and this turn's scope).
- **Decision/recommendation:** use the established accounting procedure; preserve these supplied resolutions:
  1. `component-boundary.md`: take main's Terminal-control, Containment port, Git, Run Supervisor, Launch gate and Agent protocol rows, plus new Holder-lock and Session Identity text. Keep the plan's Event Store row immediately after Git. Main's three route rows precede the plan's carriage sections.
  2. Holder-lock spec: main's sides win. `event-seal` and `event-append` are registered in `lock-registry.v1.json`; the state store's hot mutex `withStateTx` replaces `events.lock`/`claims.lock`. These two facts supersede L11-20's old wording at those spots; they do not claim Event Store implementation has shipped.
  3. Packaging-distribution `activation-binding`, `release-manifest`, `setup-doctor-registry`: main's new numbered sections precede the plan's retained-source appendices.
  4. Intent-preservation ledger: main's two new rows collided with candidate PF-I011/PF-I012, so main's rows are PF-I022/PF-I023 in the candidate. Main references them only in `plans/reports/phase-lead-261018-2025-controller-death-p06-doctor-docs-changelog.md`; reconciling that incoming reference at Phase 9 landing is a named obligation, not an edit now.
  5. Citation-drift baseline: accept main's removal of the deleted unlock-skill entry and retain the plan's two `docs/specs` entries. The baseline is untouched by this turn.
- **Accounting scope:** update only the six exception digests, append exact main commit provenance and keep their inherited approval/revisit metadata. Five files equal main's bytes; reading-map preserves the L11-25 accepted banner and two knowledge-skill fixes. This is not content approval. [Merge accounting](merge-accounting.json) records both digests and per-path main commits.
- **Reproduction scope:** enumerate exact first-parent non-merge commit/path pairs against the two existing manifest exception arrays. At input HEAD no pair is missing; leave both manifests unchanged rather than inventing a pair or changing embedded checker logic. A later runner commit needs its actual commit/path accounting, never a guessed id.
- **Unit boundary:** no carry/rebind. Phase 9's first obligation remains per-unit accounting of every changed candidate file (A47), now including the thirteen `docs/platform` files changed by this merge. The Phase 9 file contains the exact list.
- **Proof/limit:** ratchet exit 0 after data accounting. The requested three-file test run has an additional reproduction blocker: ownership audits see 85 target-digest drifts across nine targets, before any Phase 8 edit. [Exact rows](reproduction-blocker.json). Adding commit/path pairs cannot repair target bindings. Do not modify the audit or rebind units to manufacture a pass. Route this conflict to the Lead under A42; independent review is still owed.
- **Known separate blocker:** P7-DOC-GAP-05 dead-vocabulary guard is Phase 9's pre-landing obligation, not an excuse for the reproduction failure.

## Owner-only decision routing for later execution

No owner answer is needed to finish this detailing turn. The Lead settles routine link choices, ambiguities, review sizing and scope readings under A42, recording L12 decisions. A proposed new engine or expansion outside the approved scope is first analyzed by the Lead; it is not silently added.

When an excluded action becomes concrete, send one batched decision packet following `core/skills/_shared/decision-question.md`. Each question includes these five explicit parts:

- **What is happening:** identify the pinned reviewed change and the action requested (main landing/push; Phase 9 cutover/deletion; stable/public contract renumbering).
- **Cause:** show remaining evidence, consumer parity and dependency results, and why current authority cannot safely move earlier.
- **Options and cost:** execute the exact reviewed action (state touched paths/contracts, time and rollback cost), defer it (state affected rows and waiting cost), or revise scope (state rework/review cost). Supply actual estimates at that gate, not invented approval now.
- **Recommendation:** Lead chooses from the evidence and names unresolved proof; this planning document does not recommend bypassing a failed gate.
- **Scope of answer:** specify exactly which landing/push, cutover/deletion paths or contract identities are approved; no bundled permission for other excluded actions. A49 alone approves none of them.

## L12-2 — Drifted target bindings from the main merge stay with the Phase 9 accounting (superseded by L12-3)

- **Facts:** after merge `49ad04648`, the two ownership audits in `test/scripts/doc-rationale-reproduction.test.mjs` (step10-round5, step10-sync4d) reject 85 decision target bindings whose target digests drifted because main edited nine files: activation-binding (50), release-manifest (14), docs/specs/runner.md (7), setup-doctor-registry, architecture-manifest.json, confinement-authority.md, distribution.md (3 each), component-boundary and holder-lock spec (1 each). Exact rows: `reports/phase-08/reproduction-blocker.json`. No reproduction pair is missing; the ratchet is green after the six re-accounted exceptions.

### L12-11 — Separate committed-pair accounting after each author commit (N1)

Keep the established `6f88f5d8e` Phase 7 manifest-data route. Each batch has (1) its actual author/runner commit and (2) a separate accounting-only commit appending that batch’s exact `{commit,path,authority}` pairs to both Phase 6 reproduction manifests’ embedded exceptions data. No allowlist widening, checker logic edit or guessed commit id. The accounting commit touches only those allowlisted manifests; progress is written in the author batch before handback and the next author update reports the previous accounting receipt. **Batch end is after the accounting commit.** Run audits/checks on clean committed bytes there, never certify a working-tree snapshot and never make reproduction green a pre-commit runner requirement for out-of-allowlist author paths. Step 0 consumes the pinned exact pair list and re-enumerates before execution, including later plan commits and this runner commit when known. Reason: working-tree writes and then their unaccounted commits necessarily fail A until the approved data append; separating the checkpoints makes the established gate achievable without changing it. Both manifests must agree; the two historical `6f88f5d8e` pairs are the procedural precedent, not a blanket path exemption.

### L12-13 — Restore the accepted lease on current Holder-lock/Git doors (N3; supersedes L12-6)

The owner’s 2026-10-06 decision stands: deliver the lease in Phase 8 per all ten decisions in `cutover-write-lease-design.md` §6, including no TTL, fail closed, explicit abandon, rehearsal and reference-transaction guard. As refined by L12-15, Step 6b first performs a read-only reuse check against `src/holder-lock/index.mjs` `acquire` under a lease-owned registered lock name, and `docs/platform/git/spec.md` typed ports. - **Decision:** reuse the Holder-lock `acquire` facade (`src/holder-lock/index.mjs`) under its own lease lock name, with a new lock-registry row coordinated with the Holder-lock owner through the program board before build. ## A50 — Review large census classes at the mapping level (owner, 2026-10-19)

- **Question:** the third census slice (maps/portals/maintained prose) has 5,427 edges in 1,036 files that reduce to 1,018 distinct (legacy reference → owner/anchor) mappings; the per-edge A36 packet was 338 parts / 20,030 entries / ~57 MB with 3,889 mandatory deep reads.
- **Answer: option A.** For this class, and any later census class of similar shape, A36 applies at the mapping level: (1) mechanical replay of every edge (digest, line, span, mapping join) by one reviewer; (2) deep review of every distinct mapping; (3) deep review of every exception set (pending-target, pending-behavioral-proof, mechanical target failures, anchor-check failures, writers, behavioral/runtime rows); (4) ≥15% sample of edges for semantic read. The 3% rework threshold still applies per set and triggers a full read of that set. The post-cutover link check remains the backstop for deterministic mapping-driven rewrites.
- **Scope:** sampling unit only; no change to dispositions, owner-only gates, or the per-batch accounting/acceptance rules. The committed 338-part per-edge packet is superseded, not reviewed.

## L12-21 — Class-3 mapping review: systemic parser defects, dispositions and regeneration (Lead, A42)

- **Evidence:** A50 review at `2854c92ff` (author `e064f8bd5`): replay PASS on 5,427 edges / 1,018 mappings, sound for digests/spans/joins/coverage, with mutation probes failing as expected (`review-a38-step-02c-map-replay.md`); 35 deep-read parts reviewed (`review-a38-step-02c-map-part-01..35.md`: all 17 mapping parts and exception parts 18–35). Rework far above 3% in most parts, from a few repeated causes. Parts 36–97 were not dispatched: reviewing a packet with known systemic defects is waste; the packet is regenerated and re-reviewed instead.
- **Systemic defects to fix in the generator, not row by row:** (1) partial-span patches whose `old` is a directory prefix or path suffix while `new` is a full path (444 edges); (2) corrupted keys — link-label bracket glued to the path (`docs/platform/[../specs/...`), consumer-directory prefixing of relative or repo-prefixed tokens (`repo/docs/backlog.md`, `docs/architect/docs/specs/...`), unexpanded brace lists (`{p6-probe.sh,…}`); (3) the label and destination of one Markdown link split into two edges; (4) prefix matches inside a longer token (`docs/architect` inside `docs/architecture-map.md` / `docs/architecture-manifest.json`, ~95 edges); (5) code tokens in JS/minified HTML matched as paths. Fix: parse each Markdown link as one unit (label+destination; a path-text label follows its destination's owner and patch); resolve relative links against the consumer file; whole-token matching; patches always replace the complete reference; code false positives excluded with a reason.
- **Disposition rules (apply across the class):**
  - Provenance is preserved, never patched: source-locator columns of migration ledgers/inventories (e.g. `source-inventory.md` column 1, ~636 rows), "Source type"/"Provenance" headers, SHA- or blob-pinned citations ("from X at `<sha>`"), audit "historical redirect" rows, track-closeout file lists. Deleted targets get a non-executable successor note only (e.g. `verification/implementation-alignment.md` → `history/retired-engine/verification/implementation-alignment.md#literal-snapshot`).
  - Migration-state cells (switchboard status rows, `docs/platform/README.md` "Current source during migration" column) are updated by the Phase 9 authority flip with row semantics, never by path substitution.
  - Generated files (`docs/decisions/index.md`, `docs/knowledge/decisions-index/decision-record.md`, `docs/doc-registry.md`, `docs/backlog.md` from `.bee/backlog.jsonl`) are never hand-patched; retargeting goes through their generator source (Step 5 traces it).
  - Operative read instructions ("Read: X", "Read and follow X") and single-destination files map to the exact retained file, not the area README; README mapping is for plain navigation of split documents only. No patch may point into the consumer file itself or into verbatim carriage sections.
  - Exact-match contract keys (front-matter `authoritative_for`, compared by `src/report/authoritative-match.mjs`) are preserved.
  - `docs/specs/reading-map.md` references follow the frozen decision to `docs/reading-map.md`; quoted text that no longer exists in either map stays historical (`maps-01794`).
  - Verification-evidence references (file or directory) under `docs/architect/agent-coordination/verification/**` take the same-path `docs/platform/agent-coordination/verification/**` mirror prefix; live operative ones (how-to cell-trace write paths, `maps-01522..01527`, mapping-0111) are writer rows for Step 5.
  - Checker-scope statements (citation-drift default `docs/specs`, `fix-bare-citation-findings.md` command) are behavioral rows (Step 5): script default and documented command change together.
  - Upstream/external-project citations (bee, beehive, distillery reports) carry no fgOS owner and are preserved (L12-10); the ui-spec compiler's own schema/config stay at `docs/ui-spec` (not retiring), preserved.
  - Host-invocation roadmap sentences naming `docs/architect/packaging-distribution/` as the decision location follow the promoted portal `docs/platform/packaging-distribution/README.md`, flagged as a semantic rewrite for Step 3.
  - Template placeholder links (`docs/templates`, shipped) are preserved (L12-10).
- **Next:** one Doer batch regenerates the class-3 census and its mapping packet with the fixed parser and these rules, reports a before/after diff per defect class, and a fresh A50 review runs on the regenerated packet (mapping parts + every exception set + ≥15% sample), with a replay that also checks overlay precedence downstream.

A framing sentence that declares a body "retained verbatim … non-authority history / not navigation" makes that region carriage, like a Literal Snapshot heading; fenced dated upstream snapshots and "Retained … source" regions (`roadmap.md` "Retained selected source material", `spec.md` "Retained Distribution Source Detail") are carriage. Sentences about the legacy file itself ("Legacy `docs/architecture-map.md` only changes at Phase 9") and about how the legacy tree is organised are parked for Step 3.
  7. Patch form: a patched link's label follows its destination, including basename-only labels and `./` destinations; relative tokens stay relative to the consumer, repo-root tokens stay repo-root, `repo/` prefixes are kept; markdown link destinations use the GitHub slug (`githubSlug` from `scripts/list-doc-anchors.mjs`), plain `path#anchor` citations use the extractor anchor; a fenced command is decided as one unit (pasted prompt templates are not commands); a blocked candidate-only row is labelled `cutover-patch-only`, never `exact-cutover-patch`, and every parked row carries an explicit blocked/parked status so a Phase 9 action filter cannot select it.
  8. Header lists: `Related:` lists in wrapper headers outside a Literal Snapshot are live navigation and are patched, unless the patched target is the consumer itself or already an entry of the same list (lineage preserve, listed in the Phase 9 broken-link baseline). "Superseded by" preserve applies to history files only: files under `history/**` or files that declare themselves history ("Document type: History", "Superseded / implementation history only"); in live specs it is patched. "Preserved from [X]" and "source …" lineage cells are provenance.
  9. Classification details: a retired single document that exists only as carriage is parked, not pointed at the area portal; operative reads in "ĐỌC TRƯỚC"/handoff read lists park when no live exact file exists; checker scan-scope sentences (citation-drift scope, R1) are behavioral Step 5; files read at runtime (`docs/contracts/fgos-gateway-api-v1.yaml`, served by `gateway.rs` and read by `mcp.rs`) are behavioral Step 5; help text about free-form `--scope` slugs (`cli-004/008`) is a Step 5 behavioral help-text row with candidate text only; upstream-vs-fgOS labels follow the path owner; glob tokens resolve to their directory, never a missing-target failure or "Deleted historical target" note; a nearby commit pin never turns a plain pointer into provenance; retired-checkpoint instructions whose target has a live owner are patched (`maps-i-00517`, `mapping-i-0127`); brace lists expand to unique same-path mirrors; wrapped references inside `// ` code comments are joined.
  10. Broken-link population: links broken by this plan's relocation into verbatim history are labelled "broken by relocation into verbatim history", not "pre-existing"; placeholders are dropped from the baseline; findable successors are recorded (`maps-i-05313` → retired decision history `#8-adr-0012-…`). The census list is not a complete inventory: Phase 9 computes its own baseline.
- **Gaps recorded:** P8-DOC-GAP-02 — `docs/specs/enduser-docs-authoring.md`, `docs/specs/enduser-docs-index.md` (non-authority user corpus, switchboard row 61) and `docs/specs/platform-foundations.md` (curated projection) have no Phase 6 destination while Phase 9 step 6 deletes `docs/specs/**`; their rows stay parked; Step 3 chooses a destination, and a delete-without-carry goes to the owner. P8-DOC-GAP-03 — `docs/backlog.md` still says "GENERATED FILE" in its header and the switchboard says "generated, do not edit manually"; Step 5 corrects both (the Phase 9 row flip for that switchboard row carries the corrected claim).
- **Phase 9 flip notes:** owner cells naming the legacy file as owner (work-state, runner, distribution) flip to the exact `spec.md`; `enduser-docs-*`, `component-boundary` and `contracts` rows need a Step 3 flip value; rows naming paths that stay valid (`docs/contracts/`, `docs/ui-spec/`, `docs/doc-registry.*`, `docs/backlog.md`, `docs/reading-map.md`) flip status only.
- **Next:** one Doer round applying classes 1–10, regenerating as step-02j with a per-class before/after count, a probe per class, replay PASS at the committed HEAD, and mandatory re-review parts for every row whose action, target or patch text changed from 02i; then the step-02j seeded sample under the carried A50 expansion rule.

## A51 — Step 2 gate changed: the census is a mechanical inventory; exact cutover patches are authored and reviewed per consumer file in Step 3 (owner, 2026-10-19)

- **Question (Lead, decision template):** the step-02j seeded semantic sample (seed `A50-L12-26-merged-20261019-step02j-7f491cc2`, 1275 edges, 22 parts, `review-a38-step-02j-map-part-01..22.md`) failed at 106/1275 = 8.3% rework, 15 of 22 parts above 3%. Across seven regenerations (step-02c … step-02j) the mechanical layer always passed (committed-byte replay, git-tree existence, probes) while per-edge semantic judgments did not converge: each round fixed named classes and exposed new variants (locator spellings, history-vs-navigation by passage role rather than target state, alias data in prose examples, successor notes). Options given: (1) parser round 8 plus edge-level review of all ~8,500 edges; (2) change the gate — census = mechanical inventory, exact patches authored per consumer file in Step 3 with per-file diff review; (3) accept step-02j with ~8% residual. Lead recommended (2).
- **Owner answer:** "đồng ý phương án 2".
- **Rule (supersedes the per-edge patch-precomputation part of A50 for Step 2 classes 3–6; the 3% threshold and its expansion rule stay, applied to the new review unit):**
  1. Step 2 delivers, per surface class, a complete mechanical inventory of every reference edge (consumer file, line/column span, matched expression, legacy target, file digest, coarse class and role hint, carriage/history region flags, existence at the input commit), proven by committed-byte replay and git-tree existence checks. Coarse class and role are advisory input for Step 3, not acceptance-gated decisions; no per-edge exact patch text is required or reviewed in Step 2.
  2. step-02j is accepted as the class-3 inventory on its mechanical evidence (replay at the committed HEAD); its per-edge actions, owners and patch texts are advisory only. The L12-21..L12-26 rules and Lead rulings remain the authoring rules for Step 3.
  3. Step 3 authors the exact cutover patch per consumer file: an author reads the whole file and every inventory edge in it, applies the authoring rules, and emits one diff per file (plus parked/row-flip/preserve entries for edges not patched). Every inventory edge of the file must be dispositioned in that file's record (mechanical coverage check).
  4. Review unit is the per-file diff in context. A seeded sample of ≥15% of patched files (and every file above a size/edge threshold set in the Step 3 request) is reviewed first; ≤3% rework per set passes, a breach expands to every file of that set. Coverage, anchors, relative-path resolution and label form are checked mechanically on every diff.
  5. Census classes 4–6 follow the same inventory-only Step 2 gate.

## L12-30 — Step 3 pilot rejected; target-resolution procedure and authoring rulings (Lead, A42/A51, 2026-10-20)

- **Result:** full independent review of the 21-file pilot (`740375386`, author pin `ae141550c`) by six file-group reviewers ([g01](review-a38-step-03-pilot-g01.md) … [g06](review-a38-step-03-pilot-g06.md); per-file groups replaced the eight streamed packets because large files spanned packets). 7 ok / 14 rework: batch-001 1/1, batch-002 2/5, batch-040 8/11, batch-041 4/4. Mechanical checker 0 failures (rerun by Lead). Dominant defect: edges parked as "no live target" when a live target exists. Lead spot-checked at the pin: `doc-registry.json` alias for the herdr layout doc, RUL74/RUL75 in `runner/subcomponents/h-6979005beaf7/spec.md:160-162`, `decision-citation-drift/spec.md` exists, work-state §7 "Pull Door Take And Return" exists, RUL84/89/93 in runner decisions.
- **Rulings (authoring rules for every Step 3 batch from now on):**
  1. **Target resolution before any park.** For each edge that would be parked for a missing/unclear target: (a) look up the legacy path in the Phase 6 alias table and `docs/doc-registry.json` aliases (L12-26 class 2); (b) `git grep` at the author pin for every locator token in the passage — RULnn, D-ADRnnnn, ADR-nnnn, decision numbers, quoted section headings — across `docs/platform/**` (incl. subcomponents, operations, decisions, contracts, architecture, proposals) and `docs/knowledge/**`, `docs/reference/**`, `docs/tutorials/**`; (c) a live hit outside carriage is the target: patch to the exact file and anchor (Phase 9 timing). Park only when (a)+(b) find nothing live; every such park carries a `searchWitness` (queries, hit counts, why each hit is not usable). The plan-local checker must reject a park whose reason is missing-target without a witness.
  2. **Park reasons name the smallest real blocker** (the exact missing passage, decision, carry or parity proof). Boilerplate reasons are rework. Cite P8-DOC-GAP-01/02/03 only when the gap actually applies; records for `enduser-docs-*` and `docs/specs/platform-foundations.md` carry a P8-DOC-GAP-02 note.
  3. **Carriage targets.** A file whose header declares a retained source snapshot (e.g. proposals "Source snapshot retained") is carriage and never a patch target; candidate decision-record files under `docs/platform/**/decisions/` are live targets (earlier CoordinationSession ruling stands). When content lives both in a carriage heading and a live section, patch to the live section. Same-kind edges get the same disposition (08187/08188).
  4. **Illustrative placeholders** (e.g. `docs/how-to/x.md` example values) are preserved with reason "illustrative example", never parked as broken links.
  5. **Factual reasons.** A preserve/park reason must state the true target state: "retained path exists", "target never existed in git history", or "target removed at <commit> with no successor". A never-existing target in live navigation is parked as a dead link with that fact; in examples/history it is preserved.
  6. **Descriptive text must stay true.** A patch that repoints a label while leaving an adjacent description false for the new target (e.g. "5 tệp" file counts) is rework: the patch updates the description from the target's actual content, or the edge is parked with "description needs rewrite".
  7. **Bare area pointers** ("xem runner.md") patch to the candidate area spec (`docs/platform/<area>/spec.md`), Phase 9.
  8. **Default-root and CLI-default strings** describing checker/runtime behavior (e.g. `--specs-dir` default `docs/specs`) are parked on Step 5 parity, consistently.
  9. **Workflow carry (L12-27).** Links to `docs/specs/workflow.md` park with dependency "workflow carry into the candidate tree"; the `workflow.md` record states the carry obligation; its own links are authored as Phase 9 specs and re-resolved by the carry step.
- **Next:** one Doer round re-authoring the 14 rework files and re-checking the 7 ok files against rulings 1–9 (notes only where a ruling applies), with the checker witness rule and a probe for it; then full re-review of every changed record. Scaling to the remaining batches waits for a passing pilot.

## L12-31 — Step 3 pilot round 2: 18/21 ok; remaining rulings (Lead, A42/A51, 2026-10-20)

- **Result:** full re-review of round 2 (`72eccf618`, pin `ae141550c`) by the same six file groups ([r2-g01](review-a38-step-03-pilot-r2-g01.md) … [r2-g06](review-a38-step-03-pilot-r2-g06.md); r2-g03 transcribed by Lead from the reviewer's final message because the reviewer wrote no file): every round-1 defect confirmed fixed; 18 ok / 3 rework — batch-001 0/1, batch-002 0/5, batch-040 1/11 (`docs/specs/reading-map.md`), batch-041 2/4 (`docs/specs/runner.md`, `docs/specs/workflow.md`). Lead reran the checker at `d7c4a0cba`: 21 files / 437 edges, 0 failures; probes 55/55 rejected.
- **Rulings (binding for all remaining batches, applied to every pilot record now):**
  1. **Anchor precision.** A patch anchors on the most specific heading that encloses the cited claim or locator (runner RUL citations → `#business-rules`; decision locators → the decision's own heading or the nearest common parent), never on a file's H1 when a narrower holder exists (08375; 08008/08009/08016/08017; 08078/08082; the five g02 cases).
  2. **Retiring-tree preserves.** For paths under `docs/specs/**` or `docs/architect/**` a preserve reason never says "retained path"; it says "exists at author pin; tree retires at Phase 9; preserved as <retired history | lineage | self-reference>", naming a live successor when one exists (08250 → work-integration.md).
  3. **Never-existing targets.** Present-tense navigation (Pointers lists, "xem …", "tại …" in a live row or rule) to a path that never existed is parked as a dead link (08215, 08244, 08218, consistent with 08241/08349). Only a passage with an explicit dated past-action marker is preserved, and its reason quotes that marker.
  4. **Removed decision ids.** Short ids of decision records deleted at a known commit say "removed at <commit> with no successor file" (never "never existed"; 08271, 08287), plus a non-executable successor note to the D-ADR narrative holder where one exists.
  5. **Region-framed history.** A preserve resting on a region framing sentence quotes that sentence in its reason; section-based splits (08249–08252 vs 08260) are accepted on that basis.
  6. **Out-of-inventory references.** The per-file author reads the whole file; any legacy reference the inventory missed (e.g. bare basenames in `system-overview.md` L14–23, `workflow.md:639`) is added to that file's record as an `authorAddedEdge` with a disposition and counted by the coverage check. No census regeneration.
  7. **Witness assessments** are per hit group, not one boilerplate sentence per edge.
  8. **Kept as authored:** 08379 links to the work-state spec because its label names the spec; 08101 `docs/contracts/fgos-gateway-api-v1.yaml` stays preserve under L12-26(3) (its runtime read is class-6 Step 5 work); 08349 park vs 08351 preserve stands on passage role.
- **Next:** pilot round 3 fixes the three rework files and applies rulings 1–8 across all 21 records; re-review covers every changed record. Scaling to the remaining batches follows a clean round 3.

## L12-32 — Step 3 pilot round 3: outcome defects 5/494 edges; record-text classes moved into the checker (Lead, A42/A51, 2026-10-20)

- **Result:** re-review of round 3 (`532016b03`, pin `ae141550c`, 494 edges incl. 57 author-added) by the six file groups ([r3-g01](review-a38-step-03-pilot-r3-g01.md) … [r3-g06](review-a38-step-03-pilot-r3-g06.md)): every round-2 defect confirmed fixed; 15 ok / 6 rework — batch-001 0/1, batch-002 0/5, batch-040 5/11 (`docs/specs/reading-map.md`, `platform-foundations.md`, `decision-citation-drift.md`, `distribution.md`, `distillery.md`), batch-041 1/4 (`docs/specs/runner.md`). Lead reran the checker at `bade80e52`: 21 files / 494 edges, 0 failures; probes 64/64 rejected.
- **Defect split.** Outcome defects (would make an applied Phase 9 patch wrong or incomplete): 08169 plugin "12 verb wrapper (… check …)" gloss false for `claude-plugin/spec.md`; 08170 "port 8788" false (candidate spec and code default 4170); 08074 anchor null where `## Area Map` holds the claim; 08232 whole-file link where §6.2 holds the claim; missed reference `specs/runner.md:26` (runner.md L1729). Record-text defects (no effect on the diff or disposition): reasons naming a superseded anchor (08181/08182/08189/08191/08192, 08008/08009/08016/08017, 08375); "never existed" for a deleted decision (08124 → removed at `4270c0c0d`); dependency fields contradicting `blockedReason` on three added edges; "removed" for a rename (08131 → renamed at `dd4816c0f`).
- **Rulings:**
  1. **Record text becomes mechanical.** The plan-local checker must reject: a reason that names an anchor different from the record's `anchor` field; a "never existed" claim when `git log` at the pin ancestry shows the path; a "removed at <c>" claim unless `<c>` deleted the path without a same-commit rename (renames must say "renamed at <c> to <path>"); a `dependency` whose category (Step 5 parity / Phase 9 carry / description rewrite / dead link / GAP-0n / workflow carry) differs from the `blockedReason` category. Categories become an enum field; prose stays optional.
  2. **Anchor from evidence.** When a patch's proof names a target line, its anchor must equal the extractor anchor of the nearest heading enclosing that line (checker-computed); a null or whole-file anchor is allowed only when the claim spans the whole file and the record says why.
  3. **Description truth.** For every patch, the author compares each adjacent descriptive claim — counts, ports, defaults, verb/file lists, version numbers, shipped/not-shipped status — with the target passage and records the comparison; any mismatch parks the edge as "description needs rewrite" (L12-30(6)) unless the patch rewrites the description from the target. Same-row edges share the disposition (08169 aligns with the system-overview plugin park).
  4. **Coverage scanner forms.** The per-file legacy scan also matches partial-root forms (`specs/<name>.md`, `architect/<name>.md`, with optional `:<line>` or `#anchor`) and is a checker-run scan, so a missed form fails mechanically.
  5. **Witness assessments** are a per-hit-group category enum (citation-only / carriage / partial-holder / unrelated / dead) with an optional note; L12-31(7) prose requirement is replaced.
  6. **Accepted as authored:** 14 runner vocabulary parks (L1298–1315); `--decisions-dir` default parks on Step 5 (L12-30(8)); P04.md R4 preserves (dated verification payload); 08087 preserve with successor note; Related-header lineage preserves (07953, 01904); 08150 partial §3.3 holder; system-overview L14 `platform-foundations.md` park on P8-DOC-GAP-02 (the spec, not the root laws file). Out of scope: prose area names without a path token ("spec Work-State"); `docs/how-to-parallel-lanes.md` (not a legacy root).
- **Gate note:** A51's file-level counting is unchanged. Rulings 1, 2 and 4 make the record-text and coverage classes fail the checker before review, so remaining review findings are semantic.
- **Next:** pilot round 4 = checker extensions with probes, fix the six rework files, re-run rulings 1–5 over all 21 records; re-review only the six rework files plus any record whose disposition, target, anchor or diff changes.

## L12-34 — Step 3 wave 1 review; retiring-tree consumers need no patch; unit and Related rules (Lead, A42/A51, 2026-10-20)

- **Wave 1 result** (`40608e81d`, maps/portals batches 003–033, 75 files / 711 edges; wave checker at `8ab390227`: 96 files / 1,206 edges, 0 failures). Reviews: A51 sample [p01](review-a38-step-03-wave1-p01.md), [p02](review-a38-step-03-wave1-p02.md), [p03](review-a38-step-03-wave1-p03.md) — 9 files, 7 rework; Lead unpatched-file stratum [u01](review-a38-step-03-wave1-u01.md), [u02](review-a38-step-03-wave1-u02.md), [u03](review-a38-step-03-wave1-u03.md), [u04](review-a38-step-03-wave1-u04.md) — 29 files, 6 rework.
- **Rulings:**
  1. **Unpatched-file stratum (strengthens A51, never relaxes it).** The A51 sample draws only from patched files, so a set whose files are all preserve/park/row-flip was never reviewed, while the dominant pilot defect was a wrong preserve/park. Every set also gets a seeded sample of ⌈15%⌉ of its files with an empty diff and ≥1 edge (seed `A51-step03-file-v1-unpatched` NUL pin NUL set-id, rank sha256(seed NUL path), tie path; files already selected excluded). The ≤3% rule and the expansion apply to this stratum too. Wave 1 selection: [step-03-wave1-unpatched-sample.json](step-03-wave1-unpatched-sample.json).
  2. **Retiring-tree consumers get no patch.** Phase 9 switches readers (step 5) and then deletes maintained files under `docs/specs/**` and `docs/architect/**` (step 6), so patch specs for consumer files inside those trees are never applied. Such a file gets one file-level record `retiresWithTree: true` with its edge ids listed for coverage, and no per-edge disposition or diff. Exceptions keep full per-edge records: `docs/specs/workflow.md` (carried, L12-27), `docs/specs/reading-map.md` (merged into `docs/reading-map.md` before retirement), and the `docs/specs/work-state.md` RUL10 passage (L12-17 carry). Non-authority evidence payloads under those trees are relocated byte-identical and are preserved. This removes about 120 files / 1,500 edges from Step 3 authoring and review. Pilot records for the other docs/specs files and the wave-1 docs/architect records are superseded by this rule; their review defects (batch-004, batch-005, batch-009) are moot.
  3. **Unit consistency.** All edges in one migration-state unit (a table row, a bullet group under a migration/status heading, a "Canonical for … current source remains X" line) share one disposition. "Canonical for: Nothing until promotion; current source remains X" lines are phase9-row-flip. Checker: edges sharing a unit id with different dispositions fail unless the record states a per-edge reason.
  4. **Related vs provenance.** A `Related:` header entry whose target survives Phase 9 is preserved; one whose target retires is a Phase 9 patch to the live holder (`docs/platform/<area>/spec.md` or the exact holder), unless that holder is the file itself or already listed (then park as duplicate). `Provenance:` / `Source:` / imported-from lines are preserved. Applies to pilot records too (07953, 01904) and to every portal README (u04 Q1). Checker: a Related-header edge with a retiring target and disposition preserve fails.
  5. **Tree-relative and backticked sibling paths** that resolve, from the consumer's directory or its area root, to a retiring file are references and must be covered (checker scan extension). Inside retiring trees this is moot under ruling 2.
  6. **Carriage-only holders** are not patch targets (L12-30(3) reaffirmed): park. Present-tense "maintained in / stable outputs in" navigation with a live holder is patched.

## L12-35 — Wave 1 expansion review: 6/48 files rework; units and scans become checker-computed (Lead, A42/A51, 2026-10-20)

- **Result** (`6f3e4f712`, wave checker at `836cc0fe1`: 96 files / 1,233 edges, 0 failures): eleven expansion parts [x-p01](review-a38-step-03-wave1-x-p01.md) … [x-p11](review-a38-step-03-wave1-x-p11.md) reviewed every file of the breached sets plus every corrected file: 48 files, 6 rework, each 1–4 edges — `docs/reading-map.md` (missed `` `contracts/` `` L163; 08020 unit split), `docs/platform/README.md` (05112/05113), `retired-engine/README.md` (missed relative link L147), `docs/platform/architecture-map.md` (06585), `docs/README.md` (00009–00012), `docs/specs/reading-map.md` (08195 unit split). Every earlier wave-1 defect is confirmed fixed.
- **Lead rulings on reviewer findings and questions:**
  1. **Units are computed, not declared.** The unit id is derived by the checker from the source: same physical line, same Markdown table row, or same list item (including its wrapped continuation lines). Edges in one computed unit must share a disposition unless a per-edge reason names why (e.g. a retained-root cell inside a flipped row). Author-declared `unitId` is advisory only. This catches 08020/08195-type splits mechanically.
  2. **Migration-state rows flip.** A row or sentence whose subject is the migration itself ("during migration", "target …", "current source remains …", "not present yet") is phase9-row-flip even when every path in it is a retained root (00009–00012, docs-portal L32). A cell that merely names a retained authority in a non-migration table is preserved (05112/05113 in the Decision Surface table).
  3. **"Superseded by" in history files is preserved** (L12-26(8)); x-p04 D1 is overruled. History-wrapper framing prose ("see docs/specs/runner.md CoordinationSession …") is history: preserve with a successor note, uniformly across all wrappers.
  4. **Carriage-internal edges state no target-existence facts.** Their reason is "inside literal carriage (L12-26(5))" plus the carriage declaration; no "never existed" / "absent" claims, which were being resolved from the wrong base. Checker: an existence claim on an edge inside a declared carriage region fails.
  5. **Reconciled registry rows are live.** A carriage-framed table whose rows the candidate reconciled (A45 CTR renumbering: CTR010 Gateway, CTR011 confinement, CTR012/CTR013) is live candidate content; 06585 patches (Phase 9) to `docs/platform/agent-confinement/spec.md`.
  6. **Scan coverage.** The checker-run scan also matches backticked directory forms (`` `contracts/` ``) resolved from the consumer dir or the row's stated parent, and relative links inside snapshots resolved from both the wrapper location and the declared original source; a hit that resolves to a retiring or legacy path in either reading needs an edge (preserve inside carriage).
  7. **Self-path successor notes fail** in the checker (L12-34(8) made mechanical). Self-name in a file's own Purpose line is not an edge.
  8. **Anchors and labels:** 08030 → `#triage-verb`; a patch that leaves a stale section label beside a new anchor (07977, 08150 `§Dev checkout shell helpers`) must rewrite the label from the target heading or park as description-rewrite.
  9. **Kept as authored:** carriage links in packaging-distribution README (07217/07218) preserved; 08152 park vs 08142/08143 flips stands on passage role.
- **Next:** one Doer round implementing rulings 1, 4, 6, 7 in the checker with probes, applying rulings 1–8 to pilot + wave 1, then a targeted re-review of the six rework files plus any file whose outcome changes. Wave 2 (skills/prompts) starts after that re-review is clean.

## L12-60 — Step 4 wave 1 review result, systemic fixes and rulings (Lead, A42/A51, 2026-10-20)

- **Wave 1 review** (`step-04-wave1-review-request.md`, 21 parts at `f068af156`, reports `review-a38-step-04-wave1-*.md`): 16 sets. **Six files need outcome rework**: `plugins/fgOS/skills/cleanup-loop/SKILL.md` and `retro-loop/SKILL.md` (two dead `docs/explanation/why-merge-loop-recurses-into-loop-not-ck-loop.md` pointers with no slot), `AGENTS.md` (instructions-001/017/022 and 019/020 must preserve, because `docs/platform-foundations.md` is the switchboard canonical route and `docs/distribution-vision.md` is a retained source; 021 patches to `docs/platform/packaging-distribution/spec.md` as Step 3 does), `core/instructions/design-principles.md` (038 and its join 037 preserve; `docs/architecture-manifest.json` is retained), `src/cli/command-registry.mjs` (class6-l-000205 preserves) and `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (class6-l-000012 carries a reason copied from another file). Every file in sets 008, 009, 011 and 016 was reviewed, so the >3% breaches need no further expansion. All other parts are ok.
- **Three systemic causes, fixed by sweeps over all 33 batches, not piecemeal:**
  1. **Population join.** Every class-4/5/6 census edge whose consumer file is a Step 4 prose consumer (skills, prompts, instructions, task specs, headerless plugin files) and whose span is prose (not code, not a runtime string) joins the Step 4 population and gets a slot.
  2. **Dead-path census gap.** Scan every Step 4 consumer file at the author pin for `docs/...` paths that do not exist, including paths wrapped across lines. Add any occurrence without a slot as an author-added slot (dead link: park with a successor note when a holder exists; Phase 9 timing). The L12-10 conventions stay unchanged.
  3. **Copied park reasons.** Re-derive every `parked` reason, blockedReason, dependency and witness text from the slot's own source passage; known copies are "merge-into-main exemption" (4) and "RUL9/RUL11 doctor contract" (3). Re-judge the disposition where the passage supports another outcome.
- **Rulings:**
  1. A runtime-emitted string (thrown error, `refuse(...)` message, persisted rationale) is user-visible output, so it belongs to Step 5 parity (class6-l-000113, class6-l-000245, `.githooks/pre-commit` lines 491/523). Every Step 4 record lists each other census edge in its file with its Step 5 exclusion reason (`stepFiveExcluded`), including example-string literals.
  2. Archived or dated plans and reports are history class. Their unslotted legacy mentions preserve, covered by the Phase 9 literal remnant search (L12-4) and L12-29. A present-tense "lives under" inside them is history, because L12-38(6) applies to live files only.
  3. A bare filename without a path is not an edge (L12-49), and a path outside this repository is out of scope.
  4. A citation of a path that never existed follows the L12-47/L12-51 wording test: provenance wording preserves, and a "see X" navigation pointer parks as a dead link (`src/install/coexist.mjs`).
  5. `AGENTS.md` line 70, "goes into `docs/decisions/`", is a present-tense pointer to a retired directory and becomes a Phase 9 edge. The two-line A34 residual is a recorded dependency carried by the Step 7 packet. The generated `docs/decisions/index.md` preserves.
  6. Missed occurrences in a covered file (for example `docs/history/gate-bypass/CONTEXT.md` in the source, `.agents` and plugin copies) get author-added preserve rows. `.claude/skills` thin wrappers are `build:skills` outputs.

## L12-72 — Review closure and systemic admission rules (2026-10-21)

Lead rulings, verbatim from the committed records-only Doer brief:

- R1 Review method for seams 4–8 under option B: source files are window-reviewed for 100% of items (merged ±40 windows, diffs inline); generated outputs are checked per file (their references follow the source owner); hash/apply/rollback exactness stays with the checker. Review payloads are built by `step-05-seam4-window-review/pack.py` (Lead tool), not by full-evidence streaming.
- R2 A generated output's reference whose source passage is preserve gets render-join to the source owner, never an own park. A generated output may be parked only when its source owner is parked, citing that owner.
- R3 A source passage with no owning record gets an author-added source slot in Step 5 (existing authorAddedReferences mechanism); its outcome follows the usual rules (live holder => patch).
- R4 Park only with a real, file-specific blocker that names what is missing for THIS passage. Boilerplate blockers ("depends on supplied roots / export parity", "no bounded artifact/export supplied", "preserve gate bytes", a missing-proof sentence) are not blockers. A hand-written source whose target set is fixed on a retained root => preserve. The generators seam blocker applies only to generator inputs/outputs.
- R5 Reject (naming the actual non-document use) operands that construct or read no document path: root-only/scalar pass-through (requireRoot, cwd, resolve/parent of a root, parameter defaults such as `repoRoot = null`, `retiringRoots = null`), `.git` probes, `.fgos` state/event-store paths (e.g. events.jsonl), accumulators, guards, text/label joins (`lines.join`, `labels.join`), table-row serialization, clone dirs (upstreams/**), cargo/out dirs, finding data fields that only carry a path value (`path: item.path`).
- R6 Admit operands that read, write, probe or emit a document path: `git show <rev>:<docpath>`, `git rev-parse <commit>:<docpath>`, existsSync/read/write on a document path, document paths put into worker context (contextRefs), payload copy/hash over a package `files` set containing docs, and user-visible messages whose printed path comes from a fixed document literal or fixed document root (the output changes if that path moves). A message that prints a path taken only from caller data, with no fixed document root, is formatting => reject.
- R7 Same line / same passage => one admission AND one disposition across ALL items (literal–literal, literal–dynamic, dynamic–dynamic). Pick the outcome the passage's real operation warrants under R4–R6.
- R8 A preserved citation of a retired/removed decision file (docs/decisions/00NN-*.md, 0000-index.md) needs a successorNote to the live retired-decision history holder (L12-39(3); Step 4 precedent docs/platform/work-state/decisions/retired-decision-history.md#12-adr-0027 style). A comment pointer into docs/specs/** or docs/architect/** with no live holder: preserve only as dated provenance with an explicit "tree retires" witness; a module-header navigation pointer with a live holder => patch.
- R9 This plan's own gate/check scripts (scripts/check-doc-*.mjs, doc-alias-resolver, doc-binding-repair and similar): their path operations are preserve while gate bytes are frozen in Phase 8, unless a named Phase 9 obligation (Decision F / phase-09 file) changes them — then park citing that exact obligation. Never a generic blocker.

## General rulings (L12-74)

- R10 Convergence: an admission flip whose disposition stays preserve and changes no Phase 9 action is fixed now but needs no further re-review (checker rules cover it). Only disposition changes (patch/park/preserve/render-join), diffs and new slots go to re-review.

- R12 Templated blockers: a blockedReason that matches another passage's blockedReason after normalising quoted expressions, operands, ids and line numbers is boilerplate (R4). Strengthen checker rule (b) to normalise before comparing, with a rejecting probe. Every surviving park names its own concrete dependency.
- R13 Gate/frozen scripts (R9 sharpened): this plan's own gate scripts (check-doc-*, verify-phase-0x, propose-doc-decisions, generate-doc-inventory, doc-alias-resolver, doc-binding-repair) and the frozen-at-B generate-shipped-path-inventory: path operations preserve with ONE frozen-gate-identity witness per file. Exception: generate-shipped-path-inventory classifier prefixes for docs/specs or docs/architect park with the specific obligation that those prefixes change in the same Phase 9 cut (frozen B lifts only at Phase 9).
- R14 Hand-written skill/generator code with fixed non-docs roots (src/setup/skill-wrappers.mjs and similar): preserve, not generators-seam parks; Phase 9 projection regeneration is a run, not a code change. Park only an operation that touches docs/specs or docs/architect, citing that obligation.

- R16 Admission classes: existsSync/stat/git cat-file/git show on a fixed document path = probe/read => admit; a pure boolean predicate on a value = guard => reject. A user-visible message printing a fixed doc literal or a path from a fixed doc root/default (even if overridable) => admit; caller-only path => reject. Comment pointers into docs/history => admit/preserve uniformly. Split arguments of one call may differ only when they are independent operands; one expression (e.g. relative()+join('/')) has one admission.

## L12-76 — Pinned checker, residual closure and seam 5 batched authoring (Lead rulings supplied in brief; Doer, 2026-10-21)

- R17 Pins vs HEAD: every Step 5 record is checked against the bytes of its own pinned input commit (read through git objects), never against the working tree. Drift between a pinned record and HEAD is not a checker failure: the checker writes it to a non-failing head-drift list (file, record, pinned hash, HEAD hash, moved spans) that feeds Step 7 (L12-58(1)). Fix s4-pins and s4-receipts (and any other check that hashes disk) accordingly, keep a rejecting probe for a record whose pinned bytes do not match its own pin, and add a probe proving HEAD-only drift does not fail. The generator probes must start from a zero-failure baseline again.
- R18 Batched authoring for seam 5: author seam 5 in batches across turns. Order: (1) the prompt-golden atomic unit (source prompt lines 31/48 + expected golden lines 198/215, one patch unit), (2) tests that read or write real document files, (3) the rest. Each turn closes its batch fully (records, receipt entries, checker 0, probes reject) and writes step-05-seam5-continuation.json listing the remaining files in order.
- R19 Mechanical rejection with proof: you may classify a tuple as REJECT mechanically only when a checker rule proves it: the tuple's passage and its enclosing function contain no census-root literal (docs/, the retiring trees, root document names in the census), no import or identifier traced to a census-root constant, and the operand category matches a closed list (tmp/fixture dirs, .fgos state, test root, cargo/target, argv/env plumbing, string/label joins). Record the rule id and evidence on each such tuple. A probe must reject a mechanically-rejected tuple whose enclosing function contains a census-root literal. Mechanically rejected tuples are verified by the checker, not window-reviewed (option B covers admitted references and judgment calls); report their count separately.

## L12-77 — Cutover relevance triage (Lead R20; Doer, 2026-10-21)

Lead ruling, verbatim from the committed batch-3 brief:

The Phase 9 cutover changes the target of a document path only when the path is in the cutover move set: (a) anything under docs/specs/** or docs/architect/** (deleted), (b) retired decision files (docs/decisions/00NN-*.md, 0000-index.md), (c) every old path in the Phase 6 alias/registry move table (old -> new; derive it from the committed alias table and document registry, and pin it as step-05-cutover-move-set.json with its source hashes). An item whose operand provably cannot resolve to any path in that set has the same Phase 9 action whatever its label (its target survives unchanged), so by the R10 logic it is closed mechanically as cutover-neutral with disposition preserve, verified by the checker and backed by the Phase 9 full-suite proof; it needs no per-item judgment and no window review. Proof obligation (checker rule + rejecting probes): the passage and full-file context contain no literal in the move set and no prefix/template that can expand into it (a constructed path whose fixed prefix is a retained root that contains moved files is NOT neutral; neither is any identifier traced to a move-set constant, nor an unresolved import of a path constant). Everything else is judgment work under the existing rules.


Measured at immutable source pin `5eb9dc609f303fc9d54377f866b5e8caf9a5d129`: `step-05-cutover-move-set.json` derives **1,561 distinct old physical paths** from the empty adopted alias table, the committed Phase 6 merged draft (9,236 anchor/path entries, 1,031 physical paths), the document registry aliases and the retiring tree inventory. Source hashes and exact mapping provenance are embedded. Two retiring-prefix rules and the retired-decision pattern/index additionally cover absent or constructed members; 1,561 counts finite paths, not those open families. Draft aliases remain proposals, not activated aliases or writer destinations.

All **671 remaining files** are triaged. **194 files / 50 original references / 3,060 original tuples** close under `r20-cutover-neutral-whole-file-v1`, covering 1,580,425 pinned source bytes. Their 1,401 previously proved R19 tuple rejects remain verbatim as `priorR19Rejection`; the 29 earlier whole-file R19 closures and all their checker obligations are unchanged. Every R20 file has a complete-file pin/hash/mode, complete original identities, no diff and an invariant preserve disposition; it receives no invented per-item admission verdict. The checker recomputes both full-file hazards and original census operand hazards. Move-set ancestors, split constructors, static literal concatenation, open suffix/templates, traced imports and unresolved path constants stay judgment work. This is deliberately conservative: unsupported syntax is not proof of relevance or neutrality.

One further real reader closes for authoring: `test/docs/rul104-anchor-phrase.test.mjs` (six original tuples, two added source/fixture-label slots): two admissions, six judgment rejects including added slots, one parked exact-law reader, zero patches and seven preserves. Its line-26 real read must still supply the exact Business Rules RUL104 line and equality to the complete test-owned law fixture. The four-root pinned search has no matching law phrase/wording; the single RUL104 hit is an unrelated runner automatic-resume rule. The Phase 6 foundation alias does not supply that missing assertion claim. The park names the law promotion/equality obligation (P8-DOC-GAP-02), not generic missing parity. Pinned current owning tests pass 2/2; an unchanged legacy-input replay does not establish post-retirement parity.

Continuation conserves **476 files / 1,120 references / 21,805 tuples**, all three original carries unchanged. The review list contains only five cumulative judgment sources; 29 R19 and 194 R20 whole-file closures are separately listed (223 mechanical files total). The remaining queue includes 103 literal/prefix-context files, 72 additional constant/import-context files, and 301 additional open-constructor/template files; these are exclusive first-hazard counts, not semantic classifications. ## L12-91 — Rule-management lane follows cutover (Owner, 2026-10-21, option 1)

The rule-management lane ([handoff](../../../reports/handoff-261021-1021-rule-management-tight-no-conflict-right-place.md)) may analyse and design freely now, but every write to `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/platform-foundations.md` and `docs/specs/platform-foundations.md` lands only AFTER the Phase 9 cutover, on the new tree (`docs/platform/**`), because Phase 9 rewrites every reader of those files and retires `docs/specs/**`. The dependency is one-way: the cutover never waits for the rule lane; if the rule lane is unfinished or stopped, current rules migrate with the cutover like all content and the lane continues on the new tree. The ordering row in plan.md §7.5 records this decision without adding a cutover gate.

## L12-100 — Separate rejected census references and compact Step 7 outputs (Lead, A42)

No Phase 8 row is `applied-safe`: L12-9 applied no consumer edit on this
branch. The 36,191 `REJECT` + `preserve` rows are census references rather than
consumers and therefore use the separate `rejected-not-consumer` class. Every
such row must resolve a non-null `nonDocumentUseOrCustody` value from its
source record; a null is a stop. Review joins consumer rows to the four
consumer classes and counts/checks rejected rows separately. `applied-safe`
stays in the vocabulary at zero; any future row in that class requires
explicit applied-edit evidence or assembly fails.

Step 7 artifacts reference their source record, line, reference and row id;
they do not repeat source spans, diff bodies or other long record content.
Custody values and record paths use deterministic catalogs, readiness contains
per-row entries only for blocked rows plus aggregate counts, and patch order
retains diff digests and record references without copying diff text. All four
generated handoff outputs together must remain below 6,000,000 bytes while
preserving deterministic reruns and the exact final-pin command. Reason: the
handoff must distinguish non-consumers from applied edits and remain small
enough to review and transport without weakening row conservation.

## L12-104 — Step 6b slice 1 documentation-cutover lease implementation (Doer, 2026-10-22)

### 1. What was built

1. **Registry row + schema enum (D8 HARDEN coordination):**
   - Added row `documentation-cutover` at rank 10 to `packages/holder-lock/contracts/lock-registry.v1.json` with `fence: "none"`, `v1Shape: null`, `v1Path: null`, `owner: "documentation"`.
   - Added `"documentation"` to the `owner` enum in `packages/holder-lock/contracts/lock-registry.schema.v1.json`.
   - Proven green with `node --test test/holder-lock/registry-contract.test.mjs` and `cargo test -p fgos-holder-lock --test scope_rank`.

2. **Core lease module (`src/runner/documentation-cutover-lease.mjs`):**
   - `acquireDocumentationCutoverLease`: acquires kernel lock (rank 10 `documentation-cutover`), generates random 32-byte holder token outside environment, writes token to `.fgos/documentation-cutover.token`, writes atomic sidecar with token hash (never the token or session id), releases kernel lock handle (the committed sidecar carries persistent freeze across processes and holder death).
   - `inspectDocumentationCutoverLease`: inspects sidecar state, held status, age, worktree, token hash, kernel lock probe.
   - `releaseDocumentationCutoverLease`: verifies holder token possession, marks sidecar `state: 'released'`, removes token file. Refuses non-holders.
   - `abandonDocumentationCutoverLease`: refuses while kernel lock is actively held by a live process; when free, requires explicit owner confirmation (`--owner-confirmed`) and non-empty written reason (`--reason <text>`).
   - `leaseAdmission`: admission gate returning allow/refuse decisions. Refuses non-holders when lease is held, allows holders, allows out-of-scope operations. Refusal messages never print holder token or session id.
   - Fixed scope constant `LEASE_SCOPE_PATTERNS` (`docs/**`, `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `docs/transitional-switchboard.md`, `plans/260925-documentation-authority-unification/transitional-switchboard.json`, `core/skills/**`, `.agents/skills/**`, `plugins/fgOS/skills/**`, and sidecar path) and matcher (`isScopedPath`, `intersectsScope`) supporting Buffer byte paths.
   - Registered in `docs/architecture-manifest.json` as `"infra"`.

3. **Door verb:**
   - Registered `docs-lease` in `src/cli/command-registry.mjs` with subcommands `acquire`, `status`, `release`, `abandon`.
   - Dispatched in `bin/fgos.mjs` under `case 'docs-lease':`.

4. **Doctor check:**
   - Registered `documentation-cutover-lease` in `src/setup/registrations.mjs` via `checkDocumentationCutoverLeaseDoctor`. Passes when no lease is held; fails when held (reporting active lease age); fails with specific diagnostic if sidecar says held but lock file or token file is missing or mismatched.

5. **Githooks:**
   - `.githooks/pre-commit`: added documentation cutover lease guard checking staged paths against scope, refusing non-holder commits when lease is held before `hookRunsAtHome`.
   - `.githooks/reference-transaction`: new hook with fast-path for non-prepared states, non-target/plan branch ref updates, or when no lease is held. Diffing paths for updates to `refs/heads/main` and refusing non-holders when scoped paths change; refusing plan branch deletion or non-fast-forward updates.
   - Regenerated `packages/git/contracts/mutation-census.v1.json` using `node scripts/git-one-door-scan.mjs --write-census`.

6. **Owning tests:**
   - `test/runner/documentation-cutover-lease.test.mjs`: 10 passing tests proving acquire, double acquire refusal, inspect, release by holder vs non-holder, abandon refusal while held vs owner-confirmed with reason, held sidecar with lock file gone still refuses, token never in env or messages, no TTL, scope matcher with byte paths, doctor check.
   - `test/e2e/documentation-cutover-lease-hooks.test.mjs`: 8 passing tests proving pre-commit non-holder refusal, pre-commit holder allowance, pre-commit out-of-scope allowance, reference-transaction refusal for `--no-verify` commits, reference-transaction refusal for `git update-ref`, reference-transaction refusal for fast-forward merge, reference-transaction allowance for Work `.fgos` checkpoints, and ordinary commit fast path.

### 2. Test commands and real exit codes

- `env -u CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID node --test test/runner/documentation-cutover-lease.test.mjs`: exit 0 (10 pass, 0 fail)
- `env -u CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID node --test test/e2e/documentation-cutover-lease-hooks.test.mjs`: exit 0 (8 pass, 0 fail)

- `env -u CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID node --test test/e2e/root-file-guard-hook.test.mjs`: exit 0 (13 pass, 0 fail)
- `env -u CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID node --test test/setup/bwrap-check.test.mjs`: exit 0 (2 pass, 0 fail)

- `env -u CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID node --test test/setup/doctor-strict-exit.test.mjs`: exit 0 (3 pass, 0 fail)

### 3. Decisions made inside the design

- **Decision 1 (Pure filesystem main-checkout resolution):** `resolveMainCheckoutRoot` inspects `.git` directly (reading `gitdir:` in linked worktrees) instead of spawning `git rev-parse`. Reason: avoids violating the one-door rule (`scripts/git-one-door-scan.mjs` strictly forbids git spawn sites in `src/runner/`), has 0ms overhead, and operates independently of native addons.
- **Decision 2 (Direct POSIX heredoc stream in reference-transaction hook):** `.githooks/reference-transaction` processes `stdin` using a heredoc (`while ... << EOF`) rather than a piped subshell (`echo ... | while ...`). Reason: piped while loops run in subshells in POSIX `/bin/sh`, causing `exit 1` to exit only the subshell rather than aborting the hook process. The heredoc loop ensures `exit 1` directly exits the hook with non-zero status to abort Git reference transactions.
- **Decision 3 (Token outside environment and clean message redaction):** In accordance with owner decision 9, holder tokens are never read from `process.env` (preventing accidental token leakage or environment spoofing) and are strictly read from the git-ignored worktree file `.fgos/documentation-cutover.token`. All refusal messages from pre-commit, reference-transaction, and `leaseAdmission` strictly omit both the token and any holder session id.

### 4. What could not be done

- `test/setup/checks.test.mjs` contains a hardcoded list of expected check IDs for `DOCTOR_CHECKS`. Adding `documentation-cutover-lease` to `src/setup/registrations.mjs` causes that specific inventory test in `checks.test.mjs` to expect `checks.test.mjs` to be updated. Because `test/setup/checks.test.mjs` is outside the writable scope of this task slice, it was not edited, and is recorded as a write dependency (`needsWrites: ["test/setup/checks.test.mjs"]`). The check's functionality and doctor contract are fully proven in `test/runner/documentation-cutover-lease.test.mjs`.


## L12-105 — Step 6b slice 1 documentation-cutover lease rework after adversarial review (Lead under A42, 2026-10-22)

### 1. Lead rulings and implemented fixes

Closed all five High findings (H1–H5), all Medium findings (M1–M7), and all Low findings (L1–L5) from `review-a38-step-06b-slice1.md`:

1. **H1 / Ruling 1 (Authority lives on main):** The lease sidecar path is set to the plan-independent path `plans/documentation-cutover-lease.json`. Gates (`pre-commit`, `reference-transaction`, `doctor`, `leaseAdmission`) read the committed sidecar from `refs/heads/main` through the typed Git read port (`createGitInspect` / `pathsAtRevision`) and hooks via `git cat-file -e`/`-p refs/heads/main:plans/documentation-cutover-lease.json`. A working-tree copy is never trusted; untracked or forged working-tree files cannot bypass or reopen the freeze. The sidecar path itself is in the refused scope while held.
2. **H2 / M4 / Ruling 2 (One lock, one token location):** The kernel lock `documentation-cutover` always lives in the main checkout's `.fgos/` regardless of `--worktree`. The holder token lives strictly in the holder worktree's `.fgos/documentation-cutover.token`. Added `.fgos/*.token` to `.gitignore`. Main-root token fallbacks removed from both gates.
3. **Ruling 3 (Holder landings onto main):** Raw ref movement on `refs/heads/main` touching scope is refused for everyone while held, holder included. Holder landings onto main go only through Land plus the slice-2 merge gate.
4. **M3 / Ruling 4 (Abandon):** Kernel lock is not held across the holder session; abandon serializes under the kernel lock, requires explicit `--owner-confirmed` plus written non-empty `--reason`, and records `holderTokenHash` and `ageMs` in the abandon record. The undocumented `--force` flag is removed.
5. **M1 / M6 / Ruling 5 (Fail closed everywhere):** Unreadable or corrupt sidecar on `refs/heads/main` fails closed (refuses) across all gates (`pre-commit`, `reference-transaction`, `doctor`, `leaseAdmission`). Failing `git diff` in `pre-commit` or `reference-transaction` refuses while a lease exists. Fast path: when `refs/heads/main` has no sidecar (`cat-file -e` check), hooks return immediately before computing diffs.
6. **H3 / Ruling 6 (Hooks list paths with `-z --no-renames`):** `pre-commit` uses `git diff --cached --name-only -z --no-renames` without `--diff-filter` (catching renames, non-ASCII paths, and symlink typechanges). `reference-transaction` uses `git diff-tree -r -z --no-renames --name-only` and evaluates scope via the single exported `intersectsScope` helper from `src/runner/documentation-cutover-lease.mjs`.
7. **H4 / Ruling 7 (Acquire is atomic):** Acquire takes the kernel lock first, then checks whether the lease is already held under the lock, writes the holder token, and records sidecar. Proven atomic with a two-process concurrent race test (20 trials, exactly 1 winner per trial).
8. **M2 / Ruling 8 (Evidence fields):** Removed unverified `cleanWorktrees: true`. Recorded real scope digests (`tree` and `blob` OIDs from `refs/heads/main`) and `baseCommit` from `refs/heads/main`. Release compares current digests at release time against acquire-time digests and refuses if any scoped path drifted (catching `--no-verify` or `core.hooksPath=/dev/null` bypasses).
9. **L1 / L2 / M5 / Ruling 9 (No plan ids or phase labels in product code/tests; Git one-door compliance):** Reused `resolveMainCheckoutRoot` and Git Inspect port; removed all direct `.git/` hand-reading and all `execFileSync('git', ...)` calls in `src/runner/documentation-cutover-lease.mjs`. `scripts/git-one-door-scan.mjs` reports 0 sites in product code.
10. **M7 / Ruling 10 (Doctor check):** `checkDocumentationCutoverLeaseDoctor` verifies sidecar on `refs/heads/main`, kernel lock in main `.fgos/`, and `core.hooksPath` resolving `.githooks/reference-transaction`. When called from the main checkout while lease is held in a linked worktree, it reports actively held without falsely reporting "token missing".
11. **L3 / Ruling 11 (Census):** Regenerated `packages/git/contracts/mutation-census.v1.json` using `node scripts/git-one-door-scan.mjs --write-census`. Unrelated plan-script sites appear due to new verification/inventory scripts on this branch that are not yet in main's census; these are preserved as generated without hand-trimming.
12. **Ruling 12 (Red tests fixed):** Added `docs-lease` to `subcommand-flag-drift.test.mjs` MULTI array; added `documentation-cutover-lease` to `test/setup/checks.test.mjs` DOCTOR_CHECKS expected list. Both tests green.

## L12-106 — Sidecar transitions through ScopedCommit (Lead under A42; Doer delivery, 2026-10-22)

### Supplied Lead rulings

1. Supersede L12-105 ruling 1's stop-and-report clause: the existing ScopedCommit door is usable. Each successful acquire/release/owner-abandon performs exactly one declared sidecar commit in the main checkout. Order is documentation-cutover rank 10 → main-checkout rank 50 in one LockScope → preflight → committed sidecar read and scope evidence at `baseline.head` → sidecar write → ScopedCommit → release handles. Main movement refuses as `base-moved`, without an internal retry.
2. Both hooks use one Node policy helper. A main sidecar transition requires first parent = old OID, exactly the sidecar changed, a legal state edge, unchanged holder hash when leaving held, and the real main-50 borrower test. Raw main movement touching frozen scope is refused for holders too. Corrupt states, diff errors and borrower-check errors fail closed. NUL path bytes never pass through shell substitution.
3. Compute complete payload-root tree/blob OIDs at the pinned base with all listing pages; computation errors refuse acquisition. Release records `releaseScopeDigests` and `drift[]`, reports drift and still commits release. The sidecar is transition evidence rather than payload and is excluded from its own digest to avoid a self-referential digest/automatic acquisition drift; every payload root, including absent roots, has an explicit entry.
4. Owner-abandon requires confirmation and a written reason and cannot proceed while the kernel transition lock is held. A **non-holder with the CLI can abandon by design** in this single-owner repository; the owner flag is deliberate authorization, not token possession or inferred session death.
5. Doctor is not applicable outside Git, without these repository hooks, or without main. Within this repository's hook layout it checks wiring, committed state and the lock resource; it never searches for the holder token in main.
6. Token paths resolve from the registered checkout root, including calls from subdirectories. Foreign worktrees and holder checkouts nested inside frozen roots refuse. The scope list has one export; hooks and doctor consume it. Reuse `resolveMainCheckoutRoot`, Git Inspect, holder-lock and ScopedCommit, not parallel implementations. No shared switchboard constant exists in `src`; the one named `SWITCHBOARD_PATH` literal identifies that file, with no plan ID in the identifier. Its path is the necessary switchboard exception, not a second scope list.
7. Evidence must exercise the production CLI/door, not an injected sidecar committer. Before implementation, the real Git borrow probe passed for **both** hooks. No typed Git port change was needed.

### Delivery and correction of the earlier claim

L12-105's statement that production H1 was closed was incorrect: its tests injected a raw-git committer that the CLI never supplied. That callback and all its fixture uses are gone. Production transitions now use `createGitScopedCommit(...).commit(...)` with the actual main handle. The CLI JSON path also had an untested `output is not defined` error; real CLI tests exposed and fixed it by returning through the normal envelope door.

The implementation reuses the main-checkpoint adapter pattern, the existing root resolver and public holder-lock borrower test. The new hook helper supplies shared parsing, byte-path handling and transition policy; its Git adapter is read-only. Free/released pre-commit checks skip lease diffs/native resolution, while reference-transaction validates a new held transition before publication. A released sidecar therefore does not permanently disable the fast path. Runtime state writes and test Git mutations were confined to temporary fixture repositories except for the existing addon staging helper caveat below. No real lease, main landing, repository git write, worker commit, dispatch, or lifecycle mutation was performed.

### Re-review finding → owning production-path proof

`U` = `test/runner/documentation-cutover-lease.test.mjs`; `E` = `test/e2e/documentation-cutover-lease-hooks.test.mjs`. Names below identify runnable tests, not acceptance by the independent reviewer.

E `CLI release and non-holder owner abandon each commit one legal sidecar transition`: real CLI acquisition, release and abandon, one commit per transition. |
| H2, N4 | U `holder release from a subdirectory uses the checkout-root token and lands on main`; U `holder worktree must belong to this repository and its token cannot reside inside scope`. E linked-worktree holder/non-holder and CLI release from `holder/docs`. |

E `CLI release reports raw hook-disabled drift and commits the release without reopening early`; E `failed scope reads refuse CLI acquire before recording holder evidence`. |

| Main movement / transition forgery | E concurrent raw-main-move test: main advances during scope listing after real preflight, CLI returns `base-moved`, no held blob reaches main and no retry occurs. E real-main-borrower adversary test rejects held→held, hash change, extra paths, wrong parent and unknown states. |
| Raw movement coverage | E update-ref (with and without expected old), fast-forward, `--no-verify`, cherry-pick, rebase, am and pull all refuse scoped movement; the genuine `.fgos`-only checkpoint tree remains permitted. Forged working copies and `git clean -fdX` do not reopen authority. |
